This unified approach enables unbiased architectural decisions across layers with different types and sizes. HOPE is a data-free and hyperparameter-free framework. We present proof-of-concept experiments in model compression and fine-tuning to highlight the practical potential of our theory.

</details>


### [289] [Mean-to-Score Discrete Diffusion: Posterior-Mean Denoisers for Score Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21372)
*Jingyuan Li,Xiaoyi Jiang,Yixuan Jiang,Wei Liu,Yi Zhu,Zuoqiang Shi,Pipi Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Mean-to-Score (M2S) 方法，通过预测干净 token 的后验均值并经核相关线性映射得到分数，解决了 SEDD 中分数向量可能违反桥多面体约束导致采样权重为负的问题，在 CIFAR-10 和 OpenWebText 上显著提升生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SEDD 虽保证反向跳跃率非负，但不能保证 Bayes 可实现性：噪声状态下的分数比率不一定由前向核下的任意干净 token 后验联合诱导。训练得到的纯均匀 SEDD 检查点中约 1/4 的完整分数向量违反坐标盒，超过一半虽在盒内但与有效后验不兼容，导致有限步采样中出现负归一化前权重。

Method: 提出 M2S：预测干净 token 的后验均值，再通过精确的核依赖线性映射转换为分数。该构造适用于满足温和支撑条件的任意已知坐标式连续时间马尔可夫链 (CTMC)。对于均匀破坏，将概率单纯形映射到桥多面体；对于吸收掩码破坏，目标函数精确恢复 MD4。此外，将原始分数投影到桥多面体可消除负权重。

Result: 在 28.4M 参数的 CIFAR-10 对比中，M2S 将测试 BPD 从 3.173 降至 3.129，FID-50k 亦显著下降。170M 参数的 M2S 模型在约 262B OpenWebText token slots 上训练，在所有测试采样预算下均优于纯均匀 SEDD、GIDD 和 Neural CTMC 检查点，128 步采样生成 PPL 达 143.3，而最强纯均匀基线为 183.6。投影原始分数到桥多面体可将外部生成 PPL 从 203.6 提升至 175.1。

Conclusion: M2S 通过后验均值到分数的精确映射，从根本上保证了分数向量落在桥多面体内，消除了采样中的负权重问题，且在离散扩散模型上实现了最先进的生成质量，为基于分数的离散扩散提供了更 principled 的参数化方式。

Abstract: Score Entropy Discrete Diffusion (SEDD) parameterizes discrete reverse processes with unconstrained positive score ratios. While positivity guarantees nonnegative reverse jump rates, it does not ensure Bayes realizability: ratios at a noisy state need not be jointly induced by any clean-token posterior under the forward kernel. The score-entropy loss has the correct population optimum but does not enforce this constraint away from it. In a trained pure-uniform SEDD checkpoint, roughly one quarter of complete score vectors violate the coordinate box, while more than half lie inside it yet remain materially incompatible with any valid posterior. Such violations can produce negative pre-normalization weights in finite-step sampling. Projecting raw scores onto the bridge polytope removes all observed negative weights and improves external generative PPL from $203.6$ to $175.1$ without changing the sampler. We introduce \emph{mean-to-score} (M2S), which predicts a clean-token posterior mean and converts it to the score through an exact kernel-dependent linear map. The construction applies to any known coordinate-wise continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC) satisfying a mild support condition. For uniform corruption, it maps the probability simplex onto the bridge polytope; for absorbing-mask corruption, the resulting objective recovers MD4 exactly. In a controlled 28.4M-parameter CIFAR-10 comparison, M2S lowers test BPD from $3.173$ to $3.129$ and FID-50k from $\CifarSEDDFID$ to $\CifarMtwoSFID$. A 170M-parameter M2S model trained on about 262B OpenWebText token slots outperforms the evaluated pure-uniform SEDD, GIDD, and Neural CTMC checkpoints at every tested sampling budget, reaching generative PPL $143.3$ at 128 steps versus $183.6$ for the strongest pure-uniform baseline.

</details>


### [290] [A Diffusion-Model Subpopulation Digital Twin for Mobile Health Deployment: A Case Study on the HeartSteps Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21403)
*Ziping Xu,Yuyi Chang,Chenshun Ni,Nithin Sugavanam,Asim H. Gazi,Pedja Klasnja,Emre Ertin,Susan A. Murphy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出JITAI-Twins方法，利用条件时间序列扩散模型构建数字孪生，用于在真实部署前模拟目标人群并比较在线算法，通过三阶段更新（预训练、微调、推理时校准）在HeartSteps系列中验证优于简单模拟器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动健康干预越多使用在线学习和决策算法个性化推送，但设计不当的算法可能增加负担并导致参与者脱离，因此新算法设计需在真实部署前针对真实模拟用户进行验证。

Method: 基于条件时间序列扩散模型构建时间一致的数字孪生，支持三阶段信息更新：1)在大规模观测数据集上预训练；2)在相关人群的小规模先前干预部署上微调；3)推理时利用领域专家知识校准到目标人群。

Result: 在HeartSteps v2-v4系列部署中验证，该方法比简单模拟器更好地复现目标亚人群的时间结构和参与者间结构。

Conclusion: 该数字孪生可用于在部署前模拟目标部署，为测试和指导在线算法设计决策提供前提条件。

Abstract: Mobile-health interventions increasingly use online learning and decision making algorithms to personalize when to nudge users toward healthier behavior, but a poorly designed algorithm can burden and disengage participants. New algorithm design decisions should therefore be vetted against realistic simulated users before each real-life deployment. We propose a method to develop ``JITAI-Twins'': digital twins of a target subpopulation for comparing candidate online algorithms before a just-in-time adaptive intervention (JITAI) deployment. The method builds on a conditional time-series diffusion model that is temporally consistent (future actions do not affect the generated past), and it supports repeated updating from three sources of information, in three steps: pre-training on a large observational dataset, fine-tuning on small prior intervention deployments in related populations, and inference-time calibration to the next target population from domain-scientist expertise. We validate the twin at each pre-deployment stage of the long-running HeartSteps series (v2 through v4) of physical-activity suggestion intervention deployments, treating each successive deployment as an upcoming study. The proposed method reproduces the target subpopulation's temporal and between-participant structure better than simpler simulators. These results suggest that our twin can be used to simulate a target deployment before it runs, the prerequisite for testing and informing online algorithm design decisions.

</details>


### [291] [Semantic-Aware Task Clustering for Constructive and Cooperative Multi-Tasking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21426)
*Ahmad Halimi Razlighi,Maximilian H. V. Tillmann,Edgar Beck,Bho Matthiesen,Armin Dekorsy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种语义感知任务聚类方法用于协作多任务语义通信(CMT-SemCom)，通过层次密度聚类将语义对齐的任务分组，再在组内进行端到端联合训练，有效缓解破坏性合作和负迁移，提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作多任务语义通信虽能通过共享表示提升性能，但根据任务间的语义关系，合作可能是建设性的也可能是破坏性的；现有方法未区分任务语义关系，导致破坏性合作和负迁移问题，需确保建设性合作。

Method: 制定顺序多阶段优化问题：第一阶段在短暂初始训练后，利用层次密度空间聚类(HDBSCAN)进行语义感知任务聚类；第二阶段仅在发现的任务组内进行端到端(E2E)联合训练。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该框架能有效缓解破坏性合作和负迁移，相比无聚类多任务和单任务训练基线，实现了显著的准确率提升。

Conclusion: 语义感知任务聚类是实现建设性协作多任务语义通信的有效手段，通过基于语义对齐的任务分组消除负迁移，显著提升多任务协作性能。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-task semantic communication (CMT-SemCom) improves task execution performance by leveraging shared representations. However, as we demonstrated in [1], cooperative multi-tasking can be either constructive or destructive, depending on the semantic relationships among tasks. To ensure constructive cooperation, we propose a semantic-aware task clustering method for CMT-SemCom. We have formulated a sequential multi-stage optimization problem in which semantically aligned tasks are clustered once after a short initial training phase, and then end-to-end (E2E) joint training is conducted exclusively within the discovered groups. Specifically, the problem decomposes into two stages: (i) a semantic clustering problem leveraging hierarchical density-based spatial clustering, and (ii) an intra-cluster E2E CMT-SemCom learning problem. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively mitigates destructive cooperation and negative transfer, yielding accuracy gains compared to unclustered multi-tasking and individual training baselines.

</details>


### [292] [Context-weighted Discrete Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21427)
*Daniil Cherniavskii,Daniel Severo,Karen Ullrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于局部上下文的离散流匹配改进方法，通过修改连续时间马尔可夫链引入上下文加权采样器和缩放交叉熵损失，显著提升生成质量并降低困惑度，同时保持任意顺序生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准离散流匹配的因子化训练目标将可预测token与高熵模糊token混合，导致训练信号不均衡；作者发现token不确定性与其邻域上下文密度密切相关，因此引入局部上下文信息以改善采样和训练。

Method: 修改底层连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC），引入上下文加权采样器利用局部上下文密度指导采样；设计缩放交叉熵损失函数，根据token的上下文不确定性重新加权训练信号。

Result: 上下文加权采样器在可忽略计算开销下提升生成质量；缩放交叉熵损失在OpenWebText上将生成困惑度降低高达63%；方法性能媲美强半自回归块扩散基线，同时保留任意顺序生成能力。

Conclusion: 局部上下文是离散生成建模的关键因素，简单的上下文感知修改能显著提高采样和训练效率，为离散流匹配提供了实用的改进方向。

Abstract: Discrete flow matching provides a flexible framework for generative modeling on discrete structures. However, the standard factorized training objective exposes the model to targets of varying difficulty, mixing well-conditioned, predictable tokens with ambiguous, high-entropy ones. We empirically demonstrate that the uncertainty over the value of each token is closely related to the density of available context in its neighborhood. Motivated by this observation, we propose a simple modification to the underlying continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC) that incorporates local context information. Our context-weighted sampler improves generation quality with negligible computational overhead, while our scaled cross-entropy loss function reweights the training signal from different tokens and reduces generative perplexity by up to 63% on OpenWebText. Moreover, our approach matches a strong semi-autoregressive block diffusion baseline in quality while retaining the ability to perform generation in any order. These results highlight the role of local context as an important factor in discrete generative modeling and show that simple context-aware modifications can significantly improve both sampling and training efficiency.

</details>


### [293] [Error Certificates for KV-Cache Eviction via Randomized Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21475)
*Peng Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了确定性KV-cache剔除策略无法在服务时一致地估计注意力输出误差，因为被剔除的值可被任意篡改而不改变保留部分。引入随机化剔除（泊松采样尾部）并结合Hájek校正与调查抽样方差估计器，可生成每步误差证书，实证覆盖率达0.97且无精度损失。真实负载实验中七个预注册声明有三个失败：问题感知剔除在25-50%预算下近乎免费；输出对数概率比证书更能预测失败；证书门控预算升级无增益。幸存的核心价值是归因：证书能区分缓存诱导失败与固有失败（AUC 0.73-0.75，远优于输出置信度的0.47-0.54），并比随机或置信度门控更好地调度重算。随机化换来的是归因能力，而非预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定性KV-cache剔除仅保留重要性评分Top-k的token，但无法知晓被剔除部分对注意力输出的真实影响，导致服务时无法给出一致的误差估计器，亟需一种可识别且可量化误差的剔除机制。

Method: 采用随机化剔除：对尾部token按已知纳入概率进行泊松采样；在softmax内部通过一次logit偏移实现Hájek校正；利用保留集上的调查抽样方差估计器构建每步误差证书。

Result: 误差证书在实证中达到0.97覆盖率且不损失精度。七项预注册声明中三项被证伪：问题感知剔除在25-50%预算下几乎免费；输出对数概率预测失败优于证书；证书门控预算升级无额外收益。证书在归因任务上表现优异（AUC 0.73-0.75 vs 0.47-0.54），并能比随机或置信度门控更有效地调度重算。

Conclusion: 随机化剔除恢复了可识别性，使得每步误差证书成为可能，但其核心价值在于事后归因（区分缓存诱导失败与模型固有失败）而非事前预测失败。

Abstract: Deterministic KV-cache eviction keeps the top-$k$ tokens under an importance score and deletes the rest. We prove that this design cannot know what it destroyed: evicted values can be altered so that everything the serving system retains is unchanged while the true attention-output error grows arbitrarily, so no serving-time estimator of that error is consistent. Randomized eviction restores identifiability. With a Poisson-sampled tail at known inclusion probabilities, one logit offset performs the Hájek correction inside the softmax, and a survey-sampling variance estimator over the retained set becomes a per-step error certificate with 0.97 empirical coverage at no accuracy cost. On real workloads we pre-registered seven claims and lost three: question-aware eviction at 25--50\% budgets is nearly free; output log-probability predicts failure better than the certificate; certificate-gated budget escalation adds nothing. What survives is attribution: the certificate separates cache-induced from inherent failures (AUC 0.73--0.75, against 0.47--0.54 for output confidence) and schedules recomputation better than random or confidence gating. Randomization buys attribution, not prediction.

</details>


### [294] [KroQuant: Kronecker-Structured Block Transforms for Efficient Post-Training Quantization of Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21446)
*Yann Bouquet,Alireza Khodamoradi,Kristof Denolf,Mathieu Salzmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了 KroQuant，一种用于扩散变换器 W4A4 后训练量化的 Kronecker 结构可逆变换方法，在保持或提升图像质量的同时，量化精度优于 SVDQuant 和 LoRaQ，且推理速度比 SmoothQuant 快 14%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散变换器在 W4A4 量化时，激活值中的异常值导致输出质量严重下降。现有的可逆变换方法在量化精度和推理计算成本之间存在权衡：逐通道缩放虽快但损害精度；固定 Hadamard 变换精度较好但在线开销大；全维度可逆变换校准最佳但需要昂贵的稠密矩阵乘法。归一化层强制变换在每个去噪步骤在线运行，使推理计算成为关键约束。

Method: KroQuant 对激活的每个 32 元素块应用学习的 Kronecker 结构可逆变换，参数量不足逐通道缩放的一半。块局部结构作为小张量核 GEMM 运行，在 MI350 GPU 上量化器内核比 SmoothQuant 快 14%。离线 LoRaQ 权重校准吸收残余逐权重量化误差。

Result: 在 PixArt-Σ、SANA 和 FLUX.1-schnell 上以 W4A4 (MXFP4e2) 量化，KroQuant 在 MJHQ-30K 和 SDCI 基准上生成的输出比 SVDQuant 和 LoRaQ 更接近 FP 参考，同时保持或提升图像质量。

Conclusion: KroQuant 通过 Kronecker 结构可逆变换和离线 LoRaQ 校准，在极低比特量化下实现了更优的精度-效率权衡，为扩散变换器的高效部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) of diffusion transformers (DiTs) to W4A4 severely degrades output quality, because activations entering each linear layer contain outliers that 4-bit formats cannot represent. The standard fix applies an invertible linear transform to the activations and its inverse to the weights before quantizing both. Normalization layers between blocks force this transform to run online at every denoising step, making its inference computation cost the binding design constraint. Existing options trade quantization quality for inference cost: per-channel scaling (SmoothQuant) is computationally cheap but impacts the magnitude of the channels, which can harm quantization accuracy; fixed Hadamard transforms yield better quantization accuracy but require large block sizes that incur a high online cost; learned full-$d$ invertible transforms calibrate best but entail a prohibitive dense $d \times d$ matrix multiplication (GEMM) per layer per step.
  We propose KroQuant, a PTQ method that applies a learned Kronecker-structured invertible transform to each 32-element block of the activation, storing less than half the parameters of per-channel scaling. The block-local structure runs as small tensor-core GEMMs, and on an MI350 GPU the KroQuant quantizer kernel is up to $14\%$ faster than the SmoothQuant kernel. Offline LoRaQ weight calibration then absorbs the residual per-weight quantization error. On PixArt-$Σ$, SANA, and FLUX.1-schnell at W4A4 (MXFP4e2), KroQuant produces outputs closer to the FP reference than SVDQuant and LoRaQ on MJHQ-30K and SDCI, while preserving or improving image quality.

</details>


### [295] [Test-Time Scaling via Error Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21453)
*Rajiv Shailesh Chitale,Rahul Madhavan,Taneesh Gupta,Deepanway Ghosal,Aravindan Raghuveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TTEL通过token级错误定位和前缀复用，在推理时缩放计算，显著降低token生成成本并提升复杂推理任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时缩放方法（如独立采样、多轮迭代优化）缺乏token级信用分配，导致有效推理前缀被频繁丢弃，计算效率低下。

Method: TTEL利用固定或环境反馈进行token级错误定位：对比有反馈条件下的条件概率与无上下文基线，定位错误步骤，截断轨迹并分支新生成，最大程度复用有效前缀。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench上，Qwen3-8B配合TTEL达成pass@64 71.0%，token生成量仅为独立采样的一半（360.4k vs 735.0k）；在AIME-2025和HMMT-2025数学基准上，TTEL在Qwen3-8B和Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507上均稳超基线。

Conclusion: TTEL建立了严格占优的帕累托前沿，证明token级错误定位与前缀复用能大幅提升推理时计算效率，为复杂推理任务提供了一种更优的测试时缩放范式。

Abstract: Scaling inference-time computation has emerged as a reliable method to improve the performance of large language models on complex reasoning and programming tasks. However, standard approaches such as independent sampling and sequential multi-turn refinement operate without token-level credit assignment, resulting in computational inefficiency, since valid reasoning prefixes are frequently discarded. In this work, we introduce Test-Time Scaling via Error Localization (TTEL), an inference-time algorithm that utilizes fixed or environment feedback to perform token-level error localization. By comparing conditional probabilities under informed feedback against a null-context baseline, TTEL isolates the step at which an error occurred. The algorithm then truncates the trajectory and branches a new generation, maximally reusing the valid prefix. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that TTEL establishes strictly dominating Pareto frontiers across sequential reasoning domains, measured by pass-at-k vs. generated-token cost. With Qwen3-8B on LiveCodeBench, TTEL attains a pass@64 of 71.0% while generating approximately half as many tokens as independent sampling (360.4k vs. 735.0k). Generalizing to math benchmarks AIME-2025 and HMMT-2025, TTEL cleanly outperforms competing test-time baselines across both Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507.

</details>


### [296] [Windowed-MTP: Removing the Full-Context Draft-KV Tax at Million-Token Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21535)
*Alagappan Valliappan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Windowed-MTP：仅对推测解码的草稿头应用滑动窗口注意力+注意力池，将草稿KV工作集限制为常数，在1M上下文下使每解码步成本降低28%-44%，且无损目标模型输出分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原生MTP草稿头在每个草稿步对整个KV缓存执行全注意力，读取成本随上下文线性增长，在百万token上下文下成为瓶颈；草稿长度越长问题越严重，甚至导致净负收益；混合/线性注意力目标模型下验证更便宜，草稿的全注意力读取开销更凸显。

Method: 仅在草稿头的注意力上应用StreamingLLM风格的滑动窗口+注意力池，保持目标模型全注意力验证不变；训练免费、即插即用、构造上无损：全注意力目标仍决定每个被接受的token，窗口化仅改变提议哪些token，不改变接受哪些token；未读草稿KV（占总KV 7.7%-11%）通过紧凑环形缓冲区回收。

Result: 在三个架构家族（Qwen GDN-MoE 35B/122B、Mamba2-hybrid NoPE 120B）上，1M上下文单GPU SGLang测试：窗口化使每解码步成本较原生MTP草稿降低+28%到+44%，提升幅度随上下文增长；端到端解码延迟同比例改善，接受率提升处更多；完全保持目标模型验证后的输出分布。

Conclusion: Windowed-MTP是一种训练免费、即插即用、无损的方法，通过仅对草稿头施加滑动窗口注意力，将草稿KV工作集限制为常数，在长上下文推测解码中显著降低计算成本并回收显存，且不改变目标模型的输出分布。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive generation by having a cheap draft propose tokens that a target verifies in parallel. Frontier models increasingly ship a built-in Multi-Token-Prediction (MTP/NEXTN) draft head under the assumption that the draft is negligibly cheap. At million-token context this breaks: an MTP draft head typically runs full attention over the entire KV cache at every draft step, so its read grows linearly with context and comes to dominate the draft cost -- precisely where speculation is most valuable. The effect compounds with draft length (a deep native draft can turn net-negative, slower than no speculation) and sharpens under hybrid/linear-attention targets, where cheaper verification leaves the draft's full-attention read exposed. We apply a StreamingLLM-style sliding window plus attention sink to the draft's attention only (Windowed-MTP), leaving full-attention verification intact. It is training-free, drop-in, and lossless by construction: the full-attention target still decides every accepted token, so windowing changes only which tokens are proposed, never which are accepted. It bounds the draft's KV working set to a constant, dropping ~99% of KV entries at 1M. Across three architecture families (Qwen GDN-MoE 35B/122B and a Mamba2-hybrid NoPE 120B) at 1M context on a single GPU in SGLang, windowing cuts the per-decode-step cost over the shipping native MTP draft by +28% to +44%, an input-invariant margin that widens with context. Since per-token latency is this cost divided by acceptance length, at matched acceptance end-to-end decode latency improves by the same amount, and more where windowing also lifts acceptance, while preserving the target's verified output distribution. Finally, the unread draft KV -- 7.7-11% of total KV at 1M -- is reclaimed via a compact ring buffer at no acceptance or quality cost.

</details>


### [297] [Finite-Sample Coverage Audits for High-Recall Candidate Generation: Certification and Learning-Theoretic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21480)
*Martin Anthony,Kaveh Salehzadeh Nobari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究高召回率初筛阶段中，如何以有限样本有效性认证漏报相关项的质量很小，刻画了标注复杂度：证明仅使用候选集内标签无法给出非平凡界，必须采样被排除池；建立匹配的有限语料下界，指出任何有效审计在零漏报情形下需检查量级为 N0/m 的被排除池标签，确立了被排除池审计的极小极大率最优性；进而开发了基于二项与超几何分布反演的精确有限样本工具箱，可认证漏报质量、转化为召回率、同时认证预设的嵌套候选生成器族、并生成针对声明扰动机制的压力测试证书，支持结合可观测审核负担选择满足漏报目标的最低负担生成器，所有保证均要求生成器与审计规则在查看认证标签前固定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实证流水线的初始高召回阶段，漏报的相关项会永久流失至后续阶段。需要一种有限样本有效的审计方法，以最小标注成本认证漏报相关项的质量（missed mass）足够小，从而为召回率提供可靠保证。

Method: 1. 理论分析：证明仅用候选集（included pool）标签无法给出非平凡漏报界，必须采样被排除池（excluded pool）；建立有限语料下界，表明任何有效审计在零漏报时需检查 Θ(N0/m) 个被排除池标签，确立极小极大率最优性。2. 算法工具箱：基于二项分布与超几何分布的精确反演（非渐近近似），构建有限样本证书，实现漏报质量认证、通过双池设计转化为召回率认证、同时认证预设的嵌套候选生成器族、以及针对声明扰动机制的压力测试证书。

Result: 确立了被排除池审计的必要性及极小极大率最优性；提供了一套精确有限样本工具箱，能同时认证多个预设生成器的漏报质量与召回率，并结合可观测审核负担选择最优生成器；所有保证在生成器与审计规则预先固定的纪律下成立。

Conclusion: 漏报质量认证的根本瓶颈在于被排除池采样，其标注复杂度为 Θ(N0/m)；本文给出的精确有限样本工具箱实现了这一最优率，并提供了可直接用于实践的多重认证与模型选择框架。

Abstract: An initial high-recall stage in an empirical pipeline decides which items pass to later review, labelling, or modelling, and relevant items it misses are lost to every subsequent stage. We study how many audit labels are needed to certify, with finite-sample validity, that this missed relevant mass is small, and our main results characterise the label complexity of this problem. We first show that no procedure using only labels from inside the candidate set can certify any non-trivial bound on the missed mass: the audit must sample the excluded pool, the only region where unrecovered relevant items can lie. We then prove a matching finite-corpus lower bound. Any valid audit that certifies fewer than $m$ missed relevant items with high probability when none are present, even if adaptive and permitted to label the entire included pool, must inspect on the order of $N_0/m$ excluded-pool labels. Excluded-pool auditing is therefore minimax rate-optimal, not merely convenient, for missed-mass certification in the zero-miss regime. Building on this characterisation, we develop an exact finite-sample toolkit, using binomial and hypergeometric inversion rather than asymptotic approximation, that certifies missed mass, converts it to recall through a two-pool design, certifies pre-specified families of nested candidate generators simultaneously, and produces stress-test certificates against declared perturbation mechanisms. These certificates can be paired with observable review burden to select the least burdensome pre-specified candidate generator meeting a missed-mass target. Every guarantee holds under one discipline: the candidate generator, or the pre-specified family from which it is selected, and the audit rule are fixed before the certification labels are examined.

</details>


### [298] [Compact Latent Coordination for Autonomous Vehicles at Unsignalized Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21488)
*Gil Lifshits,Igal Bilik,Gilad Katz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种分层深度强化学习架构MAPS，通过中心化Master生成原型计划嵌入，解耦策略意图与战术执行，在无信号灯路口实现多车零碰撞导航并大幅缩短通行时间，且具备强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体强化学习（MARL）在无信号灯路口协调自动驾驶车辆时，面临组合动作空间爆炸、依赖特权信息、智能体设计僵化等挑战，亟需一种能解耦全局协调策略与局部执行控制、并具备泛化能力的协调框架。

Method: 提出Master-Agent Proto-plan System (MAPS) 分层DRL架构：中心化Master智能体生成紧凑连续的原型计划嵌入，编码全局协调策略；去中心化Worker智能体融合该嵌入与局部观测，执行车辆专用控制。该架构将战略意图与战术执行解耦，支持各模块独立优化。

Result: 在HighwayEnv的72种路口配置上验证，MAPS实现无碰撞导航，显著降低平均通行时间，超越最先进基线。仅用三智能体训练的系统，零样本部署到五智能体场景仍达94%成功率，证实原型计划具备强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 基于原型计划的分层强化学习为多车辆协调提供了一个极具前景的框架，有效解决了组合动作空间、特权信息依赖及僵化设计等核心难题，并展示了优异的零样本泛化性能。

Abstract: Coordinating autonomous vehicles at unsignalized intersections remains a critical challenge for multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) systems, which typically struggle with combinatorial action spaces, reliance on privileged information, or rigid agent designs. We propose Master-Agent Proto-plan System (MAPS), a hierarchical deep reinforcement learning (DRL) architecture in which a centralized Master agent generates a compact, continuous embedding, denoted as proto-plan, that encodes a global coordination strategy. Decentralized Worker agents integrate this embedding with local observations to execute vehicle-specific control, decoupling strategic intent from tactical execution and enabling independent optimization of each module.
  As a proof-of-concept evaluation of this coordination mechanism, we test MAPS across 72 intersection configurations in HighwayEnv. MAPS achieves collision-free navigation while significantly reducing average travel time, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines. The learned proto-plans further exhibit robust generalization: a system trained with three agents achieves a 94% success rate when deployed zero-shot to five-agent scenarios, confirming that proto-plan-based hierarchical learning provides a promising framework for multi-vehicle coordination.

</details>


### [299] [Zero-Flow Two-Sample Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21542)
*Yakun Wang,Leyang Wang,Song Liu,Taiji Suzuki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于零流准则的零流差异（ZFD）和零流双样本检验（ZF2ST），通过学习两分布样本的局部错位方向模式作为分布差异证据，将见证函数学习与假设评估分离，兼顾神经网络灵活性与统计校准有效性。实验表明该方法在合成及图像数据上能对结构性分布变化保持强检测功效且一类错误率校准良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双样本检验在处理结构性分布差异时往往功效不足，且难以同时兼顾灵活的见证函数学习（如神经网络）与严格的统计校准。本文旨在通过零流准则捕捉两分布样本的局部错位方向模式，构建既能利用灵活神经网络又能保持有效统计校准的双样本检验方法。

Method: 1. 提出零流差异（ZFD），基于零流准则定义统计差异度量，证明其有效性。2. 设计零流双样本检验（ZF2ST），核心思想是学习两分布样本的局部错位方向模式作为分布差异证据。3. 将见证函数学习与假设评估分离，开发基于回归和功效最大化两种见证函数学习方法，使神经网络等灵活模型可在保持统计校准前提下使用。

Result: 在合成数据和图像数据集上的实验表明，ZF2ST 能针对结构性分布变化达到强检测功效，同时一类错误率保持良好校准。相比基线方法，在复杂分布差异检测上表现更优。

Conclusion: ZF2ST 通过零流差异度量和见证学习与假设评估分离的框架，成功结合了灵活神经网络建模能力与严格统计校准保证，为结构性分布差异检测提供了一个功效强且校准有效的新工具。

Abstract: We propose a new approach to two-sample testing for deciding whether two sets of samples are drawn from the same distribution. The test is built on a statistical discrepancy based on the zero-flow criterion, termed zero-flow discrepancy (ZFD). We prove the validity of ZFD and propose a practical testing procedure, termed the zero-flow two-sample test (ZF2ST). The key idea is to learn how samples from the two distributions are locally misaligned and use the resulting directional pattern as evidence of distributional difference. By separating witness learning from hypothesis evaluation, ZF2ST can use flexible neural networks while maintaining valid statistical calibration. We develop both regression-based and power-maximized approaches for learning the witness. Experiments on synthetic and image datasets demonstrate that ZF2ST can achieve strong testing power for structured distributional changes while maintaining well-calibrated type-I error.

</details>


### [300] [X$^3$-OPD: Distilling Reasoning into Large Audio-Language Models via On-Policy Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21550)
*Dongjie Fu,Di Cao,Xize Cheng,Zihan Zhang,Wenxu Jia,Yifu Chen,Shengpeng Ji,Yu Zhang,Tao Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出跨模态在线策略蒸馏框架X^3-OPD，将文本教师的推理能力迁移给音频语言学生模型，构建三层对称语料库，实验显著提升音频推理和思维链质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型音频语言模型在听觉感知上进步大，但在深度逻辑推理上落后于文本大模型，主要缺乏高质量音频推理数据。

Method: 提出X^3-OPD跨模态在线策略蒸馏框架，学生模型基于自身声学感知生成推理轨迹，教师模型使用匹配文本输入和验证答案提供token级指导；构建三层对称语料库：文本推理渲染为语音、基于复杂声学场景的音频事件推理、涉及副语言线索的口语对话推理。

Result: 在MMSU、MMAU、BIG Bench Audio、MMAR基准上实验表明，X^3-OPD显著提升音频 grounding 推理和思维链质量，同时在领域迁移下基本保留模型现有能力。

Conclusion: X^3-OPD有效弥合音频语言模型与文本大模型在推理能力上的差距，将跨模态蒸馏扩展到非语言事件、韵律和对话语境的推理。

Abstract: While large audio-language models have achieved remarkable progress in auditory perception, they still lag behind text-based large language models in deep logical reasoning, primarily due to the scarcity of high-quality audio reasoning data. To bridge this gap, we propose X$^3$-OPD, a cross-modal on-policy distillation framework that transfers reasoning capabilities from a powerful text teacher to an audio-language student. During training, the student generates reasoning trajectories conditioned on its own acoustic perception, while the teacher provides token-level guidance using matched textual inputs and verified answers. We further construct a three-tier symmetric corpus covering textual reasoning rendered into speech, audio-event reasoning grounded in complex acoustic scenes, and spoken-dialogue reasoning involving paralinguistic cues. This design extends cross-modal distillation beyond textually recoverable content to reasoning grounded in non-linguistic events, prosody, and conversational context. Experiments on MMSU, MMAU, BIG Bench Audio, and MMAR demonstrate that X$^3$-OPD substantially improves audio-grounded reasoning and chain-of-thought quality while largely preserving the model's existing capabilities under domain shift.

</details>


### [301] [Graph Learning on Ensembles of Cyclic Peptides: An Investigation of Molecular Ensemble Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21561)
*Aaron Feller,Kris Deibler,Maxim Secor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 EnsembleEGNN，一种分子集合基础模型，通过共享 EGNN 编码每个构象，再用 Set Attention Block 池化，在 CREMP 数据集上预训练，显著提升环肽性质预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决分子性质预测通常仅使用单一代表性构象的问题，而实际分子在溶液中以构象集合形式存在，需要将热力学相关的构象集合编码为统一嵌入以提升预测性能。

Method: 采用共享的等变图神经网络（EGNN）层编码每个构象，再利用 Set Attention Block 池化构象表示；在环肽集合数据集 CREMP 上，通过多任务自监督目标（掩码 token 恢复、噪声坐标重建、成对距离重建）进行预训练；并探索与序列编码器 BERT 的联合端到端训练。

Result: 从头训练 EnsembleEGNN 完全失败（R²=0.005）；预训练模型达到 R²=0.477、Pearson r=0.699，优于仅序列的 BERT 基线（R²=0.439、r=0.667）；与 BERT 联合训练的混合模型进一步提升至 R²=0.538、r=0.737。

Conclusion: 将构象集合编码为热力学信息嵌入能显著改善环肽性质预测，证明了集合建模的有效性。

Abstract: Molecular property prediction from structure often uses a single representative conformation, even though many molecules exist as conformational ensembles in solution. We introduce EnsembleEGNN, a molecular ensemble foundation model that encodes an ensemble by first encoding each conformer with shared Equivariant Graph Neural Network (EGNN) layers, then pooling the resulting conformer representations with a Set Attention Block. We pretrain the model on CREMP, a cyclic peptide ensemble dataset, using a multi-task self-supervised objective combining masked token recovery, noisy-coordinate reconstruction, and pairwise distance reconstruction. On the CREMP-CycPeptMPDB dataset, training EnsembleEGNN from scratch fails entirely ($R^2=0.005$). However, the pretrained model reaches $R^2=0.477$ and Pearson $r=0.699$, outperforming the sequence-only BERT baseline ($R^2=0.439$, Pearson $r=0.667$). When EnsembleEGNN is co-trained end-to-end with the BERT sequence encoder, the hybrid model improves further to $R^2=0.538$ and Pearson $r=0.737$. These results demonstrate that encoding conformational ensembles into a single thermodynamically informed embedding improves cyclic-peptide property prediction.

</details>


### [302] [Beyond Sufficiency: Time Series Explanation with Counterfactual Necessity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21573)
*Hongnan Ma,Yiwei Shi,Mengyue Yang,Weiru Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TimePNS 是一种面向时间序列分类器的必要性感知解释框架，通过反事实干预识别决策关键子序列，改善充分性-必要性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于充分性的解释方法可能将高重要性分配给虚假子序列，而非模型决策所必需的部分，因此需要一种能同时评估必要性的解释方法。

Method: TimePNS 采用两阶段设计：第一阶段学习可识别的因果生成过程及充分性导向的解释掩码；第二阶段对时间因素进行反事实干预以获得必要性信号，监督时间门控细化初始解释，抑制非必要成分并强调反事实必要的成分。

Result: 在合成和真实世界时间序列基准上的实验表明，TimePNS 更准确地识别决策关键子序列，并在充分性-必要性权衡上持续优于强基线。

Conclusion: TimePNS 通过引入必要性感知机制，为时间序列分类器提供了更忠实的解释，有效解决了现有方法忽视必要性的问题。

Abstract: Faithful explanations of time-series classifiers should identify subsequences that are not only sufficient to preserve a black-box model's prediction, but also necessary for maintaining it. However, existing sufficiency-oriented methods can assign high importance to spurious subsequences that support the prediction without being essential to the model's decision. We introduce \textbf{TimePNS}, a necessity-aware framework for time-series explanation. Inspired by Pearl's counterfactual notion of necessity, TimePNS assesses whether a temporal factor is necessary by intervening on it and measuring whether the original prediction is disrupted. The framework adopts a two-stage design. Stage I learns an identifiable causal generative process together with a sufficiency-oriented explanation mask. Stage II performs counterfactual interventions on temporal factors to derive necessity signals, which supervise a temporal gate that refines the initial explanation by suppressing non-essential components and emphasizing counterfactually necessary ones. Experiments on synthetic and real-world time-series benchmarks show that TimePNS more accurately identifies decision-critical subsequences and consistently improves sufficiency-necessity trade-offs over strong baselines.

</details>


### [303] [Expanding Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21585)
*Sophia Tang,Pranam Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了扩展生成流(EFlows)和扩展流映射(EFMs)，通过在插值过程中逐步增加状态维度，实现变长序列和变尺寸图的生成，将输出大小作为可学习的可控自由度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流的生成模型局限于固定维度或固定序列长度，无法处理输出尺寸可变的生成任务，如变长序列生成和变尺寸图生成。

Method: 构建扩展插值，在状态空间中逐步引入条件噪声以增加维度；设计扩展流映射(EFMs)，将任意两个时间步之间的映射分解为扩展算子(根据当前状态增广新坐标/token)和传输映射(将扩展状态沿插值推进)，组合得到联合扩展去噪的单一映射；进一步推广到离散单纯形，支持变尺寸图和变长序列生成。

Result: 在连续和离散模态上均验证了EFlows和EFMs的有效性，建立了一种原则性框架，使输出尺寸成为可学习、可控的自由度。

Conclusion: EFlows和EFMs为变尺寸生成提供了统一的理论框架和高效的少步采样方法，扩展了流模型的适用范围，使其能够处理输出大小本身即为生成目标一部分的任务。

Abstract: Flow-based generative models have enabled remarkable progress in fast and controllable generation across continuous and discrete state spaces, yet existing parameterizations are constrained to fixed dimensions or fixed sequence lengths. Here, we introduce Expanding Generative Flows (EFlows), which define flows between distributions of increasing dimensionality along an expanding interpolant that grows the state by augmenting it with conditional noise. Building on this construction, we propose Expanding Flow Maps (EFMs), a new class of flow maps that distill the expanding interpolant into efficient few-step generative models. Each EFM factors the map between any two timesteps into two learnable operations: an expand operator, which augments the state space with new coordinates or tokens conditioned on the current state, and a transport map, which pushes the expanded state forward along the interpolant. Composing these operators yields a single map that jointly expands and denoises the state, recovering existing fixed-canvas flows and flow maps as the special case in which the expand operator is the identity. We further extend the framework to the discrete simplex, enabling variable-size graph generation and variable-length sequence generation. Across both continuous and discrete modalities, we establish EFlows and EFMs as a principled framework for settings in which output size is itself a learned, controllable degree of freedom.

</details>


### [304] [A Graph Neural Network approach to zero-shot Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20535)
*Alicia Tierz,Icíar Alfaro,David González,Elías Cueto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出零样本数字孪生框架，结合实时视觉感知与几何无关的物理信息推理引擎，基于度量辛热力学形式的热力学信息图神经网络，实现能量守恒与熵增，辅助GNN推断不可观测场，闭环数据同化修正仿真漂移，在粘弹性梁大变形与粘性流体非线性晃荡任务中实现未见几何零样本泛化，延迟约25ms/帧，支持AR投影潜在力学变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统预测性数字孪生在几何上刚性固定，当物理域或边界条件变化时需大量重训练或微调，限制了泛化能力与实时部署。

Method: 核心为基于度量辛热力学形式的热力学信息图神经网络（TiGNN），通过图消息传递局部强制能量守恒与非负熵产；辅助GNN从稀疏初始视觉边界推断不可观测场（应力张量、速度能量分布）；闭环数据同化机制：深度分割网络与稀疏光流实时跟踪宏观变形与自由表面流体边界，动态修正自回归仿真推演，消除数值漂移。

Result: 在粘弹性梁大变形与粘性流体非线性晃荡两个截然不同物理机制下，统一框架无需针对性重训练即可在新几何上实例化物理精确仿真，延迟约25ms/帧，支持增强现实直接投影潜在力学变量。

Conclusion: 该零样本数字孪生框架通过几何无关的热力学信息GNN与闭环视觉数据同化，实现了跨物理机制的极致泛化与实时性，为数字孪生在复杂动态环境中的部署提供了新范式。

Abstract: Traditional Predictive Digital Twins often remain geometrically rigid, requiring extensive retraining or fine-tuning whenever the underlying physical domain or boundary conditions change. To overcome this limitation, we present a novel framework for \textit{Zero-Shot Digital Twins} that seamlessly couples real-time visual perception with a geometry-agnostic, physics-informed reasoning engine. At the core of our architecture is the Thermodynamics-Informed Graph Neural Network architecture, a Geometric Deep Learning solver grounded in a metriplectic thermodynamic formalism that enforces energy conservation and non-negative entropy production locally through graph message passing. The framework integrates an auxiliary Graph Neural Network to infer unobservable fields (such as stress tensors or velocity and energy distributions) directly from sparse initial visual boundaries, mitigating numerical start-up transients. To bridge the sim-to-real gap, we implement a continuous closed-loop data assimilation mechanism; the pipeline tracks macroscopic deformations and free-surface fluid boundaries in real-time using deep segmentation networks combined with sparse optical flow, dynamically correcting the autoregressive simulation rollout and eliminating numerical drift. To test the validity of our approach, we demonstrate the extreme generalization capabilities of our approach across two disparate physical regimes: the large deformations of a viscoelastic beam and the non-linear sloshing of a viscous fluid. In both scenarios, the unified framework instantiates physically accurate simulations on novel, unseen geometries without case-specific retraining, operating well within real-time latency budgets (approximately 25 ms per frame) and enabling the direct projection of latent mechanical variables via Augmented Reality.

</details>


### [305] [ReliableTableQA:How Much Supervision Does Reliability Annotation Need?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20537)
*Huei-Chung Hu,Hsin-Tai Wu,Koyo Kobayashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ReliableTableQA 框架，旨在训练 LLM 标注表格问答结果的统计可靠性（而非可回答性）。构建了十类可靠性分类体系 (R1-R10)，并通过程序优先的数据管道从公开零售模式生成 5 万条带标签训练样本。实验表明，仅需 200 条模式分层的 SFT 样本即可将可靠性标记 F1 从 0.61 提升至 0.98，解析率达 1.00，将 UCAR 降为零，并能泛化至未见零售域（H&M 数据集 Rel-F1 0.997）。在强 SFT 基线下，GRPO 仅在 SFT 欠训练时（100 样本）带来 +0.06-0.16 的提升，一旦 SFT 充分则无额外收益。研究将可靠性标注重构为数据效率问题，并明确了强化微调何时有效、何时无效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有企业分析系统往往对语法正确但统计上不可靠的 SQL 查询结果（如样本量过小、置信区间过宽、混杂因素过强）给予自信回答，导致不可靠自信回答率（UCAR）居高不下。现有方法缺乏对统计可靠性的系统性标注与评估，亟需一种能自动判断计算答案统计意义的框架。

Method: 1. 设计十类可靠性分类体系 (R1-R10)，覆盖小样本聚合、多重比较膨胀、分布尾部失配等风险。2. 采用程序优先管道，基于上下文无关文法在公开零售模式上生成 5 万条带可靠性标签的训练样本，并按模式分层划分 SFT 与 GRPO 数据集。3. 通过控制实验系统研究不同监督量（SFT 样本数）对可靠性标注校准的影响，并对比 SFT 与 GRPO 在充分/欠充分监督下的表现。

Result: 仅 200 条模式分层 SFT 样本即可将可靠性标记 F1 从 0.61 提升至 0.98，解析率从 0.52 达到 1.00，UCAR 降为零，并在未见 H&M 零售域上达到 Rel-F1 0.997。GRPO 仅在 SFT 欠训练（100 样本）时带来 +0.06-0.16 的精确标记集匹配提升（域内/域外），在 SFT 充分时无可测量收益。该零结果在复合标记切片、严格全匹配指标及域外评估中均得到确认。

Conclusion: 可靠性标注本质是一个数据效率问题：高质量、模式分层的少量 SFT 数据即可实现近乎完美的泛化；强化微调（GRPO）仅在监督极度不足时有效，一旦 SFT 达标即不再提供增益。研究为企业级表格问答的可靠性保障提供了明确的数据构建与训练策略指南。

Abstract: We introduce ReliableTableQA, a framework for training an LLM to annotate the statistical reliability of tabular QA results, not whether the query is answerable, but whether the computed answer is statistically meaningful. In real enterprise analytics, a syntactically correct SQL query can return a value that is based on too small a sample, has an excessively wide confidence interval, or is too confounded to support action. Existing systems answer confidently in all such cases, a failure we quantify as the Unreliable Confident Answer Rate (UCAR). We contribute (1) a ten-category reliability taxonomy (R1-R10) covering hazards such as small-sample aggregates, multiple-comparison inflation, and distribution-tail mismatch; (2) a program-first data pipeline that generates 50,000 reliability-labeled training examples from a context-free grammar over public retail schemas, with schema-stratified SFT/GRPO splits; and (3) a controlled study of how much supervision calibrated reliability annotation actually requires. We find that a small, schema-stratified SFT set is remarkably sufficient: 200 examples raise reliability-flag F1 from 0.61 to 0.98 and parse rate from 0.52 to 1.00, drive UCAR to zero, and yield a model that generalizes to an unseen retail domain (Rel-F1 0.997 on held-out H&M). Against this strong SFT baseline, GRPO, commonly assumed to be essential, helps only when SFT is under-trained (+0.06-0.16 exact-flag-set match at 100 examples, in- and out-of-distribution) and provides no measurable benefit once SFT is adequate, a null result we confirm across a hard compound-flag slice, a strict exact-match metric, and out-of-distribution evaluation. Our findings reframe reliability annotation as a data-efficiency problem and delineate precisely when reinforcement fine-tuning does and does not pay off.

</details>


### [306] [Leveraging Biokinetic Knowledge Priors for Data-Scarce Bioprocess Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20539)
*Kyunghoon Hur,Eunjung Jeon,Hyun Woo Kim,Gyubok Lee,Seongjun Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文首次系统研究如何将生物动力学ODE先验知识注入神经网络，对比数据层面预训练与架构层面嵌入两种方式，发现两者可相互替代，仿真预训练提供了简单高效的解决方案，解决生物制造数据稀缺问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在药物发现取得显著进展，但在生物制造领域影响有限，主要原因是生物反应器实验成本高、周期长、数据稀缺且鲜少公开。尽管该领域拥有丰富的生物动力学ODE先验知识，但如何系统地将其注入神经网络尚未被研究。

Method: 提出并对比两种注入ODE先验的策略：1) 数据层面先验：在模拟的ODE曲线上预训练通用解码器；2) 架构层面先验：将ODE方程直接嵌入解码器架构。在涵盖11个数据集和7种微生物物种的实验中评估两种方法。

Result: 两种先验注入方法均显著优于无先验基线。核心发现是两者具有可替代性：在仿真数据上预训练的通用解码器，其性能可匹敌在真实数据上训练的全生物结构解码器。

Conclusion: 仿真预训练为生物过程数据稀缺条件下的深度学习提供了一个简单、数据高效的通用配方，无需复杂的架构设计即可获得同等建模性能。

Abstract: While deep learning has accelerated drug discovery, its impact on biomanufacturing has been considerably more limited. The reason is data scarcity. Bioreactor experiments are high-cost, take days to weeks, and are rarely shared in public form, leaving each research work with only a handful of experiments. The domain itself, however, is rich in prior knowledge. Biokinetic ordinary differential equation (ODE) models have described microbial growth for decades, yet how to inject this knowledge into a neural network has not been studied systematically. We present the first systematic study of how to inject this ODE knowledge into a neural network, comparing a data-level prior that pre-trains a generic decoder on simulated ODE curves against an architecture-level prior that embeds the ODE inside the decoder. Both consistently outperform no-prior baselines across 11 datasets and 7 microbial species. Our central finding is that the two are substitutable. A generic decoder pre-trained on simulation matches a fully bio-structured decoder trained on real data. Simulation pre-training therefore offers a simple, data-efficient recipe for deep learning under bioprocess data scarcity.

</details>


### [307] [HypNO: A Graph-Based Neural Operator with Physics-Informed Message Passing for Hyperbolic Conservation Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20541)
*Dimitrije Ždrale,Cassie An Jeng,Katie Wang,Sonia Vanier,Alexandre Bayen,Hossein Nick Zinat Matin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于图的神经算子HypNO，用于求解标量双曲守恒定律，通过物理信息消息传递在时空图上捕捉激波和不连续解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算子学习方法难以同时处理全局输运和激波形成，尤其是LWR和ARZ交通流模型等具有挑战性的双曲守恒定律问题。

Method: 构建有限体积单元的时空图，采用邻接分解的物理信息消息传递机制，保证上风格式和熵可容许性，从而准确捕捉激波附近的解行为。

Result: 在LWR和ARZ交通流模型上基准测试显示，HypNO能在多种初始条件下准确预测解快照，并有效捕捉激波和不连续性。

Conclusion: HypNO作为一种基于图的神经算子，通过物理信息图消息传递成功解决了标量双曲守恒定律中的激波捕捉难题，为算子学习在复杂物理系统中的应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: We introduce HypNO, a graph-based neural operator for scalar hyperbolic conservation laws. HypNO operates directly on a space-time graph of finite-volume cells and uses adjacency-factored, physics-informed message passing to respect upwinding and entropy admissibility near shocks. We benchmark the architecture on the Lighthill-Whitham-Richards (LWR) and Aw-Rascle-Zhang (ARZ) traffic-flow models, a stress test for operator-learning methods because of their simultaneous global transport and shock formation. HypNO predicts solution snapshots accurately across a range of initial conditions while capturing the shocks and discontinuities of the solution.

</details>


### [308] [SOAP, Muon, and Beyond: Pushing LLM Pretraining Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20548)
*Mikail Khona,Aditya Vavre,Boxiang Wang,Deyu Fu,Hao Wu,Mike Chrzanowski,Bryan Catanzaro,Dheevatsa Mudigere,Jeff Pool,Michael Lightstone,Mohammad Shoeybi,Mostofa Patwary,Nima Tajbakhsh,Tijmen Blankevoort*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了Muon和SOAP等高阶优化器在大规模LLM预训练中的计算成本和数值稳定性问题，提出了逐步QR正交化和改进预条件策略，实现了大批量下的稳定训练，并开发了兼容Megatron-LM的逐层分布式优化器，实验证明在万亿token规模上SOAP和Muon持续优于AdamW。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶优化器（如Muon、SOAP）虽收敛更快，但其计算开销和数值稳定性挑战限制了大规模采用；现有方法在大批量下存在损失尖峰、不稳定等问题，缺乏公平的跨优化器学习率迁移基准，且缺乏高效的大规模分布式实现。

Method: 1) 识别SOAP在大批量下的不稳定性，提出逐步QR正交化和改进预条件策略以消除损失尖峰。2) 采用update-RMS匹配进行SOAP、Muon、AdamW的统一实证研究，确保公平学习率迁移。3) 实证评估Muon的正交化质量。4) 设计兼容Megatron-LM的逐层分布式优化器，平衡内存、隐藏通信、避免近似计算。5) 引入系统级改进加速逐层实现。

Result: 在多十亿参数、万亿token训练中，SOAP和Muon在测试规模下持续优于AdamW；在高达1亿token批量的下一词预测任务中，二者保持训练稳定性和质量，而AdamW性能下降；逐层分布式实现高效扩展且保留收敛优势。

Conclusion: 通过算法改进和系统级优化，本文使高阶预条件梯度方法在大规模LLM预训练中变得实用且高效，释放了其收敛优势，并开源代码库支持社区进一步研究。

Abstract: Higher-order optimizers such as Muon and SOAP offer faster convergence than AdamW, but their computational cost and numerical stability challenges have limited adoption at scale. In this work, we adapt and enhance preconditioned gradient methods to overcome the practical challenges of large-scale LLM pretraining. We first identify instabilities in SOAP at large batch sizes and propose algorithmic modifications including per-step QR orthogonalization and improved preconditioning strategies that eliminate loss spikes and enable stable training in these regimes. We then present a unified empirical study of SOAP, Muon, and AdamW using update-RMS matching to ensure fair learning rate transfer across optimizers. As part of this analysis, we empirically evaluate the orthogonalization quality of Muon. Our experiments on multi-billion-parameter models trained on trillions of tokens reveal that SOAP and Muon consistently outperform AdamW at the scales we tested. Notably, at batch sizes of up to 100M tokens for next-token prediction, these optimizers maintain training stability and quality while AdamW degrades. To enable efficient training at large scale, we introduce a layer-wise distributed optimizer compatible with Megatron-LM. Our implementation balances memory and hides communication while avoiding approximations to the optimizer computations, thus retaining their convergence benefits. Additionally, we identify and build specific system-level improvements to further accelerate our layer-wise implementation. To support the research community, we release a codebase that contains emerging algorithms for optimization: https://github.com/NVIDIA-NeMo/Emerging-Optimizers

</details>


### [309] [SenCos-GEM: SENet-Calibrated and Law-of-Cosines-Constrained Geometry-Enhanced Molecular Representation for Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20551)
*Tianming Han,Li Zhang,Qi Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SenCos-GEM，一种显式解耦的几何增强分子表示学习框架，通过余弦定律约束的物理引导几何一致性损失获取高保真 3D 空间先验，并引入 SE 模块和双调制预测头实现动态特征重校准，在 MoleculeNet 基准上建立新的 SOTA，尤其在 3D 构象敏感回归任务上显著降低误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 3D GNN 的自监督学习方法缺乏显式物理约束，易受粗糙经验力场引入的几何噪声影响；且在下游适配阶段忽视动态特征调制，导致灾难性遗忘和负迁移。

Method: SenCos-GEM 包含三大核心组件：1) 基于余弦定律的物理引导几何一致性损失，提供数学不变的 3D 空间先验；2) 轻量级 Squeeze-and-Excitation (SE) 模块作为任务特定适配器集成到骨干网络；3) 结合 Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM) 和 SENet 机制的双调制预测头，实现动态特征重校准。

Result: 在 MoleculeNet 基准上表现极具竞争力，在 3D 构象敏感回归任务上建立新 SOTA：FreeSolv RMSE 降低 12.9%，Lipophilicity RMSE 降低 5.3%，QM9 MAE 降低 8.2%。模型在立体异构体区分和构象扰动判别上展现出优越的空间建模能力。

Conclusion: SenCos-GEM 通过显式物理约束和动态特征调制，有效解决了几何噪声敏感和负迁移问题，实现了高精度分子性质预测，为药物发现中的分子表示学习提供了重要突破。

Abstract: Effective molecular representation learning is crucial for accurate molecular property prediction. Recently, numerous self-supervised learning (SSL) approaches leveraging 3D GNNs have been developed to capture comprehensive 3D structural information for drug discovery. However, existing methods lack explicit physical constraints and are highly susceptible to geometric noise induced by coarse empirical force fields during large-scale pre-training.Furthermore, they overlook dynamic feature modulation during downstream adaptation, often resulting in catastrophic forgetting and negative transfer. To address these limitations, we introduce SenCos-GEM, a novel explicitly decoupled geometry-enhanced molecular representation learning framework that incorporates SENet-calibrated and law-of-cosines-constrained enhancements. SenCos-GEM employs a physics-guided geometric consistency loss based on the law of cosines to derive high-fidelity and mathematically invariant 3D spatial priors. In addition, lightweight Squeeze-and-Excitation (SE) modules are integrated into the backbone as task-specific adapters, while a dual-modulation prediction head combines Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM) and SENet mechanisms to enable dynamic feature recalibration. SenCos-GEM demonstrates highly competitive performance across diverse classification and regression tasks on MoleculeNet benchmark, establishing new state-of-the-art results specifically on 3D conformation-sensitive regression tasks, such as FreeSolv, Lipophilicity, and QM9, achieving relative error reductions of 12.9% (RMSE), 5.3% (RMSE), and 8.2% (MAE), respectively. Moreover, our model exhibits superior capability in distinguishing stereoisomers and discriminating conformational perturbations, underscoring its robust spatial modeling performance. Collectively, SenCos-GEM represents a significant breakthrough in accurate molecular property prediction.

</details>


### [310] [Monkey King Bang: A Unified Scientific Multimodal Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20557)
*Hesen Chen,Xinyu Su,Xiaomeng Yang,Yuetan Lin,Zixiong Yang,Junyi An,Fenglei Cao,Yifeng Jiao,Yunqi Zhang,Yuan Cheng,Zhiyu Tan,Hao Li,Libo Wu,Yuan Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 MKB，一个统一的科学多模态模型，采用共享 Transformer 骨干网络与模态定制化编码器、适配器和解码器，覆盖 DNA、RNA、蛋白质、小分子、地球科学和医学影像六大科学领域，支持生物序列、分子字符串、气象场和分割掩码等原生输出。通过两阶段“模态-语言”课程训练，实现跨领域理解、推理与生成，并在多个基准上表现优异，同时保留了 Qwen3-VL 的通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前科学发现趋向多领域推理，但现有 AI 系统要么针对单一领域专用，要么主要通过文本标记化和提示接口统一科学数据，难以处理多样化科学输入、产出模态原生输出，以及支持跨领域的联合理解、推理与生成。因此需要一种统一的多模态模型来解决这些局限。

Method: MKB 基于共享 Transformer 骨干网络，为每个模态配备定制化的编码器、适配器和解码器。训练采用两阶段课程：第一阶段冻结骨干网络，对齐各模态专用组件；第二阶段解冻骨干网络，融合混合科学语料与通用语料进行联合训练。模型覆盖六大科学分支，支持序列、字符串、场和掩码等原生输出。

Result: 实验表明，MKB 在生物与分子基准上达到有竞争力的理解性能，在天气预报、生物生成和医学图像分割任务上产出高保真原生输出，且基本保持了 Qwen3-VL 骨干的通用能力。结果验证了共享骨干加模态定制组件的范式可行性。

Conclusion: MKB 展示了统一科学多模态模型的可行性，为未来跨领域科学多模态探索提供了有前景的基础。模型与代码已公开发布。

Abstract: Scientific discovery is increasingly shifting from isolated disciplines to multi-domain reasoning, and AI for science faces a similar transition. Existing systems are either specialised for individual domains or unify scientific data mainly through text tokenisation and prompt-based interfaces, limiting their ability to handle diverse scientific inputs, produce modality-native outputs, and support joint understanding, reasoning, and generation across scientific domains. We introduce MKB, a unified scientific multimodal model for both understanding and generation, built around a shared Transformer backbone and modality-tailored encoders, adapters, and decoders. MKB covers six scientific branches, including DNA, RNA, proteins, small molecules, earth science, and medical images, and supports native outputs such as biological sequences, molecular strings, meteorological fields, and segmentation masks. Training follows a two-stage modality-then-language curriculum: Stage 1 aligns modality-specific components with the frozen backbone, and Stage 2 consolidates them with the language backbone using mixed scientific and general corpora. Experiments show that MKB achieves competitive scientific understanding across biological and molecular benchmarks, produces high-fidelity native outputs for weather forecasting, biological generation, and medical-image segmentation, and largely retains the general capabilities of its Qwen3-VL backbone. These results demonstrate the feasibility of the proposed paradigm, suggesting that shared-backbone models with modality-tailored components can provide a promising foundation for future cross-domain scientific multimodal exploration. The model and code are publicly available at https://github.com/Shanghai-Academy-of-AI-For-Science/MKB and https://huggingface.co/sais-org/MKB.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [311] [Probabilistic Residual Learning for Online Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20863)
*Wenyuan Wang,Yusong Zhao,Zihao Xu,Hengyi Wang,Qi Xu,Zhigang Hua,Yan Xie,Yi Wang,Zihao Zhao,Bo Long,Chengzhi Mao,Shuang Yang,Hengguan Huang,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出概率残差学习(PRL)，一种因果贝叶斯推荐模型，通过建模真实值与基础预测之间的残差，实现对现有深度学习推荐系统的针对性改进。PRL 结合用户概率分组、领域级混淆因子建模和 do-calculus 聚合，作为即插即用模块兼容多种基础模型，并在实验中显著提升性能且自动发现有意义的用户簇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代推荐系统多基于深度学习的稠密编码器，存在黑盒性质和计算复杂度高的问题，难以系统性地增强推荐能力。

Method: 提出 Probabilistic Residual Learning (PRL)：（1）概率性地将用户分组以进行局部残差建模；（2）建模影响用户和物品表示的领域级混淆因子；（3）利用 do-calculus 在混淆因子上聚合簇特定的残差预测。

Result: 实验表明，PRL 作为即插即用模块兼容多种基础深度学习推荐系统，能够提升其性能，并自动发现有意义的用户簇。

Conclusion: PRL 通过因果贝叶斯建模残差，有效解决了深度学习推荐系统黑盒难改进的问题，实现了性能提升与可解释性用户聚类的双重优势。

Abstract: Modern recommender systems are typically based on deep learning (DL) models, where a dense encoder learns representations of users and items. As a result, these systems often suffer from the black-box nature and computational complexity of the underlying models, making it difficult to systematically enhance their recommendation capabilities. To address this problem, we propose Probabilistic Residual Learning (PRL), a causal Bayesian recommendation model that models the residual between ground-truth and base predictions, enabling targeted refinement of existing systems. Specifically, PRL (1) probabilistically groups users for localized residual modeling, (2) models domain-level confounders that influence user and item representations, and (3) aggregates cluster-specific residual predictions over the confounders using do-calculus. Experiments demonstrate that our plug-and-play PRL is compatible with various base deep learning recommender systems, improving their performance while automatically discovering meaningful user clusters.

</details>


### [312] [LO-FAR: A Cost-Aware Local Filter for Sparse Feature Ranking in Industrial Ad Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20873)
*Egemen Erbayat,Luis Duque,Sohini Roychowdhury,Mohammad Amin,Srihari Reddy*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 LO-FAR（Localized Feature Ranking），一种仅需 CPU、与模型无关的工作流，利用轻量级局部估计器对工业广告推荐中的高基数稀疏 ID-list 特征进行排序，避免了昂贵的 GPU 重训练循环。在百万级交互、475 个特征的生产数据集上，LO-FAR 约 2 个 CPU 小时完成排序，在 CTR/CVR 任务上保留的 Normalized Entropy 收益与 shuffle-based importance、Binary Stochastic Neurons 等交互感知方法相当，证明了在算力与迭代周期受限时，简单的局部过滤器是实用的生产选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业广告推荐模型严重依赖稀疏、高基数的 ID-list 特征，每个特征对应一个独立的 embedding 表，导致存储、训练和服务成本极高。随着流量和下游模型演进，特征排序不仅是离线建模问题，更是受限于计算预算和迭代节奏的反复系统决策。现有的排列重要性、随机门控等方法需要 GPU 重训练，周期长、成本高，亟需一种低成本、快速周转的替代方案。

Method: LO-FAR 采用纯 CPU、模型无关的流程：对每个候选特征单独计算其在保留集上的预测信号，使用轻量级局部估计器（如单特征逻辑回归或梯度提升树）快速评估特征的独立预测能力，避免了全模型重训练和特征交互建模。排序结果直接用于决定保留哪些特征（预算 100–400 个），无需考虑特征间交互。

Result: 在超过 100 万交互日志和 475 个稀疏 ID-list 特征的生产数据集上，LO-FAR 约 2 个 CPU 小时完成全量特征排序。在 CTR 和 CVR 任务上，保留不同预算下的特征子集，其 Normalized Entropy 收益与 shuffle-based importance、Binary Stochastic Neurons、基于覆盖率的启发式方法等交互感知基线具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 当计算预算和迭代周期是主要约束时，基于局部独立预测信号的简单过滤器（LO-FAR）能以极低成本、极快速度给出特征排序，且下游指标损失可控，是比重型交互感知方法更务实的生产部署选择。

Abstract: Industrial ad recommendation models rely heavily on sparse, high-cardinality ID-list features that encode user histories and contextual identifiers. Each is backed by a dedicated embedding table, so these features dominate storage, training, and serving cost and must be revisited as traffic and downstream models evolve. Therefore, sparse feature ranking is not just an offline modeling problem but also a recurrent systems decision limited by compute budgets and iteration cadence. We present Localized Feature Ranking (LO-FAR), a CPU-only, model-agnostic workflow that ranks each candidate feature from its stand-alone held-out predictive signal using lightweight local estimators rather than the GPU-bound retraining loops of permutation- and stochastic-gate-based methods. On a production dataset of more than one million logged interactions and 475 sparse ID-list features, LO-FAR completes ranking in approximately two CPU-hours and preserves downstream Normalized Entropy gains on CTR and CVR tasks that are competitive with shuffle-based importance, Binary Stochastic Neurons, and a coverage-based heuristic across budgets of 100--400 retained features. The contribution is a deployable workflow showing that, when cost and turnaround constraints are binding, a simple local filter can be a practical production choice over heavier interaction-aware alternatives.

</details>


### [313] [Controllable and Content-Based Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20938)
*Fırat Öncel,Jihoon Jeong,Emiliano Penaloza,Mirco Ravanelli,Laurent Charlin,Cem Subakan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出CCBR框架，利用文本用户画像实现可控推荐，通过文本瓶颈实现多模态干预，性能媲美潜在表示模型且优于TEARS基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐系统依赖潜在稠密表示，难以解释和控制，需要可控且基于内容的推荐方法。

Method: CCBR框架基于文本用户画像构建推荐，接入协同过滤模型，引入文本瓶颈实现可控性，直接从物品内容（图像、音频、视频）推断文本摘要。

Result: 在图像、音频、视频数据集上，性能与标准潜在表示模型竞争，提供可控文本摘要，优于TEARS基线，系统干预实验验证用户引导机制有效。

Conclusion: CCBR实现了可控的基于内容的推荐，兼顾性能与可解释性，通过文本瓶颈支持多模态干预和用户引导。

Abstract: Traditional recommendation systems rely on latent (dense) representations, making them difficult to interpret and control. We propose the Controllable and Content-Based Recommendations (CCBR) framework, which builds its recommendations from textual user profile representations. CCBR plugs into collaborative filtering models and introduces controllability via text bottlenecks. We show that CCBR enables text-based and multimodal interventions, allowing users to steer the model towards the directions they prefer. Different from existing controllable recommendation systems, CCBR infers the text summaries directly from item contents (images, audio or video). Across image-, audio-, and video-based datasets, we demonstrate that the proposed framework obtains competitive model performance with standard (latent-representation) models while providing controllable model summaries via text. The model also outperforms TEARS, a recent baseline for controllable recommendation systems. Through systematic interventions, we demonstrate the efficacy of the user steering mechanism.

</details>


### [314] [Fast and Efficient Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search for High-Dimensional LLM Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20957)
*Nico Hezel,Kai Uwe Barthel,Bruno Schilling,Konstantin Schall,Andre Moelle,Klaus Jung*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文介绍了 SISAP 2026 索引挑战的两个任务解决方案：任务1针对1024维 BGE-M3 嵌入的 kNNG 构建，任务2针对未归一化 Llama-3.2-8B 特征的 MIPS 搜索。核心创新包括：利用等体积 Voronoi 多面体（EVP）量化加速构建并配合重排序保持高召回；将 MIPS 通过维度增扩转为欧几里得搜索；引入基于快速线性分配排序（FLAS）的一维预排序机制，显著提升图遍历时的空间局部性和缓存命中率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在严格约束下基准测试近似最近邻搜索（ANNS）算法，需同时优化构建速度、查询延迟、内存访问效率与召回率。现有方法在高维稠密向量（如 BGE-M3、Llama 特征）上难以平衡这些指标，尤其 MIPS 问题的不对称性和图索引的缓存不友好特性限制了性能。

Method: 1. 任务1（kNNG构建）：采用等体积 Voronoi 多面体（EVP）进行高效量化，辅以针对性重排序策略保持高召回。2. 任务2（MIPS）：通过维度增扩将非对称内积问题转化为欧几里得距离搜索。3. 通用优化：图构建前引入基于快速线性分配排序（FLAS）的一维预排序，改善数据空间局部性，提升后续图遍历的缓存命中率并降低查询延迟。

Result: 在 SISAP 2026 挑战的两个任务中，所提方法有效平衡了构建速度、查询延迟与召回率。EVP 量化加速了索引构建；维度增扩使 MIPS 高效转化为欧几里得搜索；FLAS 预排序显著改善了内存访问模式，提升了图遍历的缓存命中率并降低查询延迟。源码已开源。

Conclusion: 论文提出的 EVP 量化、MIPS 欧几里得转化及 FLAS 预排序三大技术协同作用，为高维稠密向量的 kNNG 构建与 MIPS 搜索提供了高效、低延迟且高召回的解决方案，验证了在严格基准约束下优化空间局部性与量化策略的有效性。

Abstract: The annual SISAP Indexing Challenge benchmarks Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search (ANNS) algorithms under rigorous constraints. This paper presents our submissions for the 2026 edition, addressing both $k$-Nearest Neighbor Graph (kNNG) construction on 1024-dimensional BGE-M3 embeddings (Task 1) and Maximum Inner Product Search (MIPS) on unnormalized Llama-3.2-8B features (Task 2). To optimize construction speed, we utilize Equi-Voronoi Polytopes (EVP) for efficient quantization, supplemented by targeted reranking strategies to maintain high recall. For MIPS, we transform the asymmetric inner product problem into a Euclidean search space via dimensionality augmentation. To reduce query latency and optimize memory access, we introduce a 1D presorting mechanism via Fast Linear Assignment Sorting (FLAS) prior to graph construction. This significantly improves spatial locality and cache hit rates during subsequent graph traversal.
  Source Code: https://github.com/Visual-Computing/sisap26-deglib

</details>


### [315] [Bridging the Structural Gap: Adapting Autoregressive Generation for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21028)
*Junchao Zeng,Junzhang Zhu,Junyang Chen,Yudong Li,Wei Liu,Chengxiang Zhuo,Zang Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 BARGE 模型，通过 Item Context-Aware Attention (ICA) 恢复项目级结构，以及 Hierarchical Path Reranking (HPR) 和 Dual-Path Decoding (DPD) 抑制语义漂移，解决了生成式推荐中的结构鸿沟，在离线和在线实验中均显著提升推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐 (GR) 将项目编码为层级语义 ID 并逐 token 预测，但存在两个结构鸿沟：1) 将多 token ID 展平为单一序列破坏了项目级结构；2) 训练与推理在层级码本上的不一致导致语义漂移。

Method: 提出 BARGE 模型：编码阶段使用 Item Context-Aware Attention (ICA) 恢复项目级结构；解码阶段结合 Hierarchical Path Reranking (HPR) 和 Dual-Path Decoding (DPD) 从两个互补角度抑制语义漂移。

Result: 在公开基准和大规模离线测试中，BARGE 取得优越推荐性能；腾讯平台在线 A/B 测试显示点击率提升 0.60%、点击独立访客提升 1.34%、总阅读时长提升 1.70%。

Conclusion: BARGE 有效弥合了生成式推荐与推荐任务的结构鸿沟，在工业级推荐系统中具有显著实用价值。

Abstract: Generative Recommendation (GR) has emerged as a new paradigm for sequential recommendation, in which a representative line of work encodes items into hierarchical semantic IDs via residual quantization and predicts the IDs token by token. However, this generative formulation still exhibits structural gaps with respect to the recommendation task: flattening multi-token IDs into a single sequence destroys item-level structure, and the inconsistency between training and inference over a hierarchical codebook gives rise to semantic drift. To bridge these two gaps, we propose BARGE, which employs Item Context-Aware Attention (ICA) to restore item-level structure during encoding, and Hierarchical Path Reranking (HPR) together with Dual-Path Decoding (DPD) to suppress semantic drift from two complementary angles during decoding. Extensive experiments and analytical studies on public benchmarks and a large-scale offline test demonstrate that BARGE achieves superior recommendation performance. An online A/B test on a Tencent platform yields improvements of 0.60% in click-through rate, 1.34% in click unique visitors, and 1.70% in total reading time, confirming the practical value of BARGE in industrial-scale recommendation.

</details>


### [316] [SHIFT: Self-reconstruction Harnesses Implicit Fine-grained Thinking for Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21333)
*Yuxiao Luo,Da Li,Mingjie Zhang,Zhentao He,Shikun Zhang,Wei Ye*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出SHIFT（自重构利用隐式细粒度思维进行检索），一种基于LLM的检索训练框架，通过残差投影和任务导向双向注意力聚合将LLM转化为推理高效的检索器，并利用细粒度下一词预测重构缓解对比学习与隐式推理的目标不匹配，在推理密集型检索基准上持续超越现有检索器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的检索器（显式推理如rewrite-then-retrieve，隐式推理如GIRCSE和LaSER）在推理密集型检索基准上表现竞争力，但难以解决检索目标（对比学习）与生成目标之间的不匹配问题。

Method: SHIFT框架包含两大核心组件：1）通过残差投影和任务导向双向注意力聚合在潜在空间中将LLM转化为推理高效的检索器；2）利用细粒度下一词预测的自重构机制，缓解对比学习与隐式推理之间的目标不匹配。

Result: 在推理密集型检索基准上的大量实验表明，SHIFT持续优于其他广泛使用的检索器。同时进行了详细分析说明方法的工作原理。

Conclusion: SHIFT通过自重构和隐式细粒度思维有效解决了检索与生成目标的不匹配问题，在推理密集型检索任务上取得了优越性能。

Abstract: LLM-based retrievers have become a fundamental component of modern information retrieval systems. The paradigm of "rewrite-then-retriev" introduces explicit reasoning before retrieval. In addition, implicit-reasoning retrievers such as GIRCSE and LaSER improve efficiency by replacing explicit reasoning with soft tokens. Although these methods demonstrated competitive performance on reasoning-intensive retrieval benchmarks, they struggle to address the mismatch between the objectives of retrieval and generation. In this work, we propose SHIFT ($\textbf{S}$elf-reconstruction $\textbf{H}$arnesses $\textbf{I}$mplicit $\textbf{F}$ine-grained $\textbf{T}$hinking for Retrieval), a retrieval training framework based on LLMs. Firstly, we transfer LLMs into reasoning-efficient retrievers with residual projection and task-oriented bidirectional attention aggregation in the latent space. Secondly, we alleviate the mismatch between contrastive learning and implicit reasoning using fine-grained next-token-prediction-based reconstruction. Extensive experiments on reasoning-intensive retrieval benchmarks show that SHIFT consistently outperforms other widely used retrievers. We also carried out a detailed analysis to illustrate how our method works.

</details>


### [317] [Diffusion Language Model for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21519)
*Chengyi Liu,Yongqi Zhou,Junwei Pan,Zhixiang Feng,Chengguo Yin,Haijie Gu,Jie Jiang,Yinghao Liu,Yujuan Ding,Qing Li,Wenqi Fan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 DLMRec，一种面向推荐的离散扩散语言模型，旨在解决自回归生成在推荐任务中的局限性。通过协作感知随机分词器、课程驱动训练策略和稳定性感知投票机制三大核心组件，DLMRec 实现了更符合推荐场景的结构化偏好建模与鲁棒生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的推荐系统多采用自回归范式，但该范式存在两大缺陷：一是下一个词预测目标过度强调序列顺序，忽视用户偏好背后的结构化项间依赖；二是前缀约束生成限制了双向上下文，导致左向右解码时早期错误无法纠正并不断累积。

Method: DLMRec 引入三大关键组件：1) 协作感知随机分词器，将多跳协作信号编码为兼容扩散建模的表达性离散 token；2) 课程驱动训练策略，通过逐步的项级与 token 级学习，使去噪过程与偏好恢复对齐；3) 稳定性感知投票机制，聚合迭代预测以提升生成的一致性与鲁棒性。

Result: 实验表明 DLMRec 在多个推荐基准上显著优于自回归基线，验证了离散扩散语言模型在生成式推荐中的有效性。

Conclusion: DLMRec 为推荐任务提供了一个有吸引力的非自回归生成替代方案，通过扩散语言建模更好地捕捉用户偏好的结构化依赖，并通过迭代去噪与投票机制实现了更鲁棒的推荐生成。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-empowered recommender systems have emerged as a promising paradigm for generative recommendation, leveraging their strong semantic reasoning and generative capacity to model complex, diverse user preferences. However, most existing approaches rely on an autoregressive paradigm that is suboptimal for recommendation. The next-token objective emphasizes sequential order rather than the structural inter-item dependencies underlying user preferences. In addition, prefix-constrained generation restricts bidirectional context and commits to left-to-right decoding, causing early errors to accumulate without correction. Inspired by the success of diffusion language models, we propose \textbf{DLMRec}, a discrete diffusion language model tailored for recommendation that offers a compelling alternative to autoregressive generation. Specifically, DLMRec introduces three key components to bridge diffusion language modeling with recommendation. First, a collaborative-aware stochastic tokenizer encodes multi-hop collaborative signals into expressive discrete tokens compatible with diffusion modeling. Second, a curriculum-driven training strategy aligns the denoising process with preference recovery through progressive item- and token-level learning. Third, a stability-aware voting mechanism aggregates iterative predictions to improve generation consistency and robustness.

</details>
